While the fine-tuned model achieves approximately 47% pass@1 on Qiskit-HumanEval, recent general-purpose models reach 60-65% under zero-shot and retrieval-augmented settings, and up to 85% for the strongest evaluated model when combined with iterative execution-feedback agents -representing an improvement of more than 20% over zero-shot general-purpose performance and more than 35% over the parameter-specialized baseline.
  Agentic execution feedback yields the most consistent improvements, albeit at increased runtime cost, while RAG provides modest and model-dependent gains. These findings indicate that performance gains can be achieved without domain-specific fine-tuning, instead relying on inference-time augmentation, thereby enabling a more flexible and maintainable approach to LLM-assisted quantum software development.

</details>


### [199] [Chimera: Latency- and Performance-Aware Multi-agent Serving for Heterogeneous LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22206)
*Kangqi Ni,Wenyue Hua,Xiaoxiang Shi,Jiang Guo,Shiyu Chang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chimera是一个用于异构LLM集群上多智能体工作流服务的预测调度系统，通过语义路由、输出长度预测和负载均衡，在延迟和任务性能之间取得更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务系统假设同构集群，忽略了异构部署的潜力。异构部署允许不同大小和能力的模型，可以在延迟和性能之间进行更精细的权衡，但异构性带来了调度挑战。

Method: Chimera采用语义路由估计每个模型的置信度分数，预测工作流剩余输出长度，使用飞行中预测令牌量估计每个模型的拥塞情况进行负载均衡。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理的智能体工作流上评估，Chimera在可比设置中达到了最佳的延迟-性能边界，端到端延迟降低1.2-2.4倍，任务性能平均提高8.0-9.5个百分点。

Conclusion: Chimera通过预测调度有效解决了异构LLM集群上的多智能体工作流服务问题，显著改善了延迟和任务性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent applications often execute complex tasks as multi-stage workflows, where each stage is an LLM call whose output becomes part of context for subsequent steps. Existing LLM serving systems largely assume homogeneous clusters with identical model replicas. This design overlooks the potential of heterogeneous deployments, where models of different sizes and capabilities enable finer trade-offs between latency and performance. However, heterogeneity introduces new challenges in scheduling across models with diverse throughput and performance. We present Chimera, a predictive scheduling system for multi-agent workflow serving on heterogeneous LLM clusters that jointly improves end-to-end latency and task performance. Chimera applies semantic routing to estimate per-model confidence scores for each request, predicts the total remaining output length of the workflow, and estimates per-model congestion using in-flight predicted token volumes for load balancing. We evaluate Chimera on representative agentic workflows for code generation and math reasoning using multiple heterogeneous LLM configurations. Across comparable settings, Chimera traces the best latency-performance frontier, reducing end-to-end latency by 1.2--2.4$\times$ and improving task performance by 8.0-9.5 percentage points on average over competitive baselines including vLLM.

</details>


### [200] [SPA: A Simple but Tough-to-Beat Baseline for Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22213)
*Kexian Tang,Jiani Wang,Shaowen Wang,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPA是一种简单但有效的知识注入方法，通过精心设计的提示词生成大规模合成数据，在专业领域知识注入任务中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在专业、数据稀缺领域的知识覆盖不完整，需要研究合成数据生成进行知识注入。现有方法存在多样性崩溃和提示词调优不足等问题。

Method: 提出SPA方法：使用少量精心设计的提示词生成大规模合成数据，通过简单的提示工程和直接的大规模数据增强进行知识注入。

Result: SPA在系统比较中优于多个强基线方法。研究发现RL方法在小规模时token效率高但随数据规模扩大出现多样性崩溃，多阶段提示方法在精心调优后优势消失。

Conclusion: 对于知识注入任务，精心设计的提示词结合直接的大规模数据增强效果显著，SPA可作为该领域未来研究的强基线。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are pretrained on massive amounts of data, their knowledge coverage remains incomplete in specialized, data-scarce domains, motivating extensive efforts to study synthetic data generation for knowledge injection. We propose SPA (Scaling Prompt-engineered Augmentation), a simple but tough-to-beat baseline that uses a small set of carefully designed prompts to generate large-scale synthetic data for knowledge injection. Through systematic comparisons, we find that SPA outperforms several strong baselines. Furthermore, we identify two key limitations of prior approaches: (1) while RL-based methods may improve the token efficiency of LLM-based data augmentation at small scale, they suffer from diversity collapse as data scales, leading to diminishing returns; and (2) while multi-stage prompting may outperform simple augmentation methods, their advantages can disappear after careful prompt tuning. Our results suggest that, for knowledge injection, careful prompt design combined with straightforward large-scale augmentation can be surprisingly effective, and we hope SPA can serve as a strong baseline for future studies in this area. Our code is available at https://github.com/Tangkexian/SPA.

</details>


### [201] [Decoupling Exploration and Policy Optimization: Uncertainty Guided Tree Search for Hard Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22273)
*Zakaria Mhammedi,James Cohan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将探索与利用明确分离的新范式，在探索阶段绕过强化学习，使用树搜索策略和认知不确定性度量来高效探索，然后在发现的轨迹上使用监督学习训练可部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流方法使用强化学习训练具有内在动机的智能体，将探索和利用结合在一个复合目标中。作者认为这种方法存在不必要的开销：虽然策略优化对于精确任务执行是必要的，但仅为了扩展状态覆盖而使用这种机制可能是低效的。

Method: 提出明确分离探索与利用的新范式：1）探索阶段：使用受Go-With-The-Winner算法启发的树搜索策略，结合认知不确定性度量来系统驱动探索，完全绕过强化学习；2）利用阶段：使用现有的监督反向学习算法将发现的轨迹提炼成可部署的策略。

Result: 1）在困难的Atari基准测试中，探索效率比标准内在动机基线高一个数量级；2）在Montezuma's Revenge、Pitfall!和Venture等游戏中，通过蒸馏发现的轨迹获得了大幅领先的最先进分数；3）在高维连续动作空间的MuJoCo Adroit灵巧操作和AntMaze任务中，直接从图像观察解决了稀疏奖励设置，无需专家演示或离线数据集。

Conclusion: 通过明确分离探索与利用，并绕过强化学习进行探索，该方法实现了更高效的探索，能够解决具有挑战性的稀疏奖励任务，为自主探索提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: The process of discovery requires active exploration -- the act of collecting new and informative data. However, efficient autonomous exploration remains a major unsolved problem. The dominant paradigm addresses this challenge by using Reinforcement Learning (RL) to train agents with intrinsic motivation, maximizing a composite objective of extrinsic and intrinsic rewards. We suggest that this approach incurs unnecessary overhead: while policy optimization is necessary for precise task execution, employing such machinery solely to expand state coverage may be inefficient. In this paper, we propose a new paradigm that explicitly separates exploration from exploitation and bypasses RL during the exploration phase. Our method uses a tree-search strategy inspired by the Go-With-The-Winner algorithm, paired with a measure of epistemic uncertainty to systematically drive exploration. By removing the overhead of policy optimization, our approach explores an order of magnitude more efficiently than standard intrinsic motivation baselines on hard Atari benchmarks. Further, we demonstrate that the discovered trajectories can be distilled into deployable policies using existing supervised backward learning algorithms, achieving state-of-the-art scores by a wide margin on Montezuma's Revenge, Pitfall!, and Venture without relying on domain-specific knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate the generality of our framework in high-dimensional continuous action spaces by solving the MuJoCo Adroit dexterous manipulation and AntMaze tasks in a sparse-reward setting, directly from image observations and without expert demonstrations or offline datasets. To the best of our knowledge, this has not been achieved before.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Decorrelation, Diversity, and Emergent Intelligence: The Isomorphism Between Social Insect Colonies and Ensemble Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20328)
*Ernest Fokoué,Gregory Babbitt,Yuval Leventhal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文建立了蚂蚁群体决策与随机森林学习之间的数学同构框架，提出两者都遵循"随机化相同智能体+多样性增强机制→涌现最优性"的通用原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会性昆虫群体和集成机器学习方法是自然界和计算领域中两个最成功的去中心化信息处理范例。研究者希望揭示这两种看似不同的系统背后的共同数学原理。

Method: 建立严格的数学框架，证明蚂蚁群体决策与随机森林学习在"随机集成智能"的共同形式化下是同构的。使用贝叶斯推断、多臂老虎机理论和统计学习理论工具进行分析。

Result: 证明了蚂蚁通过局部线索的随机响应和正反馈实现功能分化的机制，精确对应决策树的bootstrap聚合和随机特征子采样。推导了蚂蚁招募率与树权重、信息素轨迹强化与袋外误差估计、法定人数感知与预测平均之间的显式映射。

Conclusion: 集体智能（无论是生物的或人工的）都源于一个通用原理：随机化的相同智能体加上多样性增强机制，最终导致涌现的最优性。这一同构表明自然界和计算中的集体智能遵循相同的数学基础。

Abstract: Social insect colonies and ensemble machine learning methods represent two of the most successful examples of decentralized information processing in nature and computation respectively. Here we develop a rigorous mathematical framework demonstrating that ant colony decision-making and random forest learning are isomorphic under a common formalism of \textbf{stochastic ensemble intelligence}. We show that the mechanisms by which genetically identical ants achieve functional differentiation -- through stochastic response to local cues and positive feedback -- map precisely onto the bootstrap aggregation and random feature subsampling that decorrelate decision trees. Using tools from Bayesian inference, multi-armed bandit theory, and statistical learning theory, we prove that both systems implement identical variance reduction strategies through decorrelation of identical units. We derive explicit mappings between ant recruitment rates and tree weightings, pheromone trail reinforcement and out-of-bag error estimation, and quorum sensing and prediction averaging. This isomorphism suggests that collective intelligence, whether biological or artificial, emerges from a universal principle: \textbf{randomized identical agents + diversity-enforcing mechanisms $\rightarrow$ emergent optimality}.

</details>


### [203] [Forward and inverse problems for measure flows in Bayes Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20329)
*S. David Mis,Maarten V. de Hoop*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究贝叶斯-希尔伯特空间中概率测度的正问题和反问题，建立了动力学实现理论，并提出了从时变观测数据中重建概率测度路径的变分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究贝叶斯-希尔伯特空间中概率测度的动力学行为，为处理时变概率测度的正问题和反问题提供统一的几何框架，特别是在从间接观测数据中重建演化规律方面。

Method: 1. 正问题：通过加权诺伊曼问题将log密度变化转化为最小动能梯度速度场，建立规范动力学实现；2. 反问题：在贝叶斯-希尔伯特路径空间上构建变分问题，结合数据拟合项和传输作用，并添加时空正则化以保证紧致性。

Result: 证明了最小化元的存在性，推导了一阶变分公式，建立了观测映射的局部稳定性，并在紧致性理论提供的强拓扑下恢复了演化规律、其得分和规范速度场。

Conclusion: 该研究为贝叶斯-希尔伯特空间中的概率测度动力学提供了完整的几何框架，既解决了正问题的规范实现，又为从时变观测数据中反演概率测度演化提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study forward and inverse problems for time-dependent probability measures in Bayes--Hilbert spaces. On the forward side, we show that each sufficiently regular Bayes--Hilbert path admits a canonical dynamical realization: a weighted Neumann problem transforms the log-density variation into the unique gradient velocity field of minimum kinetic energy. This construction induces a transport form on Bayes--Hilbert tangent directions, which measures the dynamical cost of realizing prescribed motions, and yields a flow-matching interpretation in which the canonical velocity field is the minimum-energy execution of the prescribed path.
  On the inverse side, we formulate reconstruction directly on Bayes--Hilbert path space from time-dependent indirect observations. The resulting variational problem combines a data-misfit term with the transport action induced by the forward geometry. In our infinite-dimensional setting, however, this transport geometry alone does not provide sufficient compactness, so we add explicit temporal and spatial regularization to close the theory. The linearized observation operator induces a complementary observability form, which quantifies how strongly tangent directions are seen through the data. Under explicit Sobolev regularity and observability assumptions, we prove existence of minimizers, derive first-variation formulas, establish local stability of the observation map, and deduce recovery of the evolving law, its score, and its canonical velocity field under the strong topologies furnished by the compactness theory.

</details>


### [204] [Operator Learning for Smoothing and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20359)
*Edoardo Calvello,Elizabeth Carlson,Nikola Kovachki,Michael N. Manta,Andrew M. Stuart*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为数据驱动方法在数据同化和预测中的平滑问题建立了首个通用逼近定理，通过结合映射存在性证明和算子学习架构分析，为连续时间设置下的神经网络算子方法提供理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习为数据驱动的数据同化和预测算法开辟了新前沿，但这些方法与模型驱动算法相比缺乏理论分析。本文旨在解决这一不足，为数据驱动方法建立理论基础。

Method: 建立理论框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 证明待学习映射的存在性；(2) 分析用于逼近该映射的算子学习架构的特性。在连续时间设置下使用神经网络算子架构，结合这两个组件进行分析。

Result: 建立了首个用于动态系统平滑和预测的纯数据驱动算法的通用逼近定理。通过Lorenz '63、Lorenz '96和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky动态系统的实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为数据驱动方法在数据同化和预测中的平滑问题提供了坚实的理论基础，通过结合映射存在性证明和算子学习架构分析，填补了该领域理论分析的空白。

Abstract: Machine learning has opened new frontiers in purely data-driven algorithms for data assimilation in, and for forecasting of, dynamical systems; the resulting methods are showing some promise. However, in contrast to model-driven algorithms, analysis of these data-driven methods is poorly developed. In this paper we address this issue, developing a theory to underpin data-driven methods to solve smoothing problems arising in data assimilation and forecasting problems. The theoretical framework relies on two key components: (i) establishing the existence of the mapping to be learned; (ii) the properties of the operator learning architecture used to approximate this mapping. By studying these two components in conjunction, we establish the first universal approximation theorem for purely data-driven algorithms for both smoothing and forecasting of dynamical systems. We work in the continuous time setting, hence deploying neural operator architectures. The theoretical results are illustrated with experiments studying the Lorenz `63, Lorenz `96 and Kuramoto-Sivashinsky dynamical systems.

</details>


### [205] [Comprehensive Description of Uncertainty in Measurement for Representation and Propagation with Scalable Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20365)
*Ali Darijani,Jürgen Beyerer,Zahra Sadat Hajseyed Nasrollah,Luisa Hoffmann,Michael Heizmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出使用高斯混合模型(GMMs)作为概率密度函数的通用近似器，用于表示和传播测量系统中的定量属性，相比传统高斯框架提供更准确的不确定性传播，同时保持计算可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率论已成为量化不确定性的主要框架，但在控制理论、制造和测量系统中广泛依赖简单高斯假设会导致复杂现象的不完整表示和多阶段损失性近似，特别是在多阶段过程中不确定性传播不准确。

Method: 提出使用高斯混合模型(GMMs)作为概率密度函数的通用近似器，其复杂度可调以平衡近似精度与内存和计算需求。GMMs是熟悉的高斯框架的原则性扩展，能够在许多情况下实现高性能和闭式解。

Result: 在制造和测量环境（特别是循环工厂）的实际应用中，GMMs框架支持测量不确定性的准确表示和传播，相比传统高斯框架提供改进的精度，同时保持计算可行性。

Conclusion: 高斯混合模型提供了一个全面且计算可行的框架，用于表示和传播测量系统中的定量属性，能够更准确地处理不确定性传播问题，同时考虑到软件系统中有限内存的约束。

Abstract: Probability theory has become the predominant framework for quantifying uncertainty across scientific and engineering disciplines, with a particular focus on measurement and control systems. However, the widespread reliance on simple Gaussian assumptions--particularly in control theory, manufacturing, and measurement systems--can result in incomplete representations and multistage lossy approximations of complex phenomena, including inaccurate propagation of uncertainty through multi stage processes.
  This work proposes a comprehensive yet computationally tractable framework for representing and propagating quantitative attributes arising in measurement systems using Probability Density Functions (PDFs). Recognizing the constraints imposed by finite memory in software systems, we advocate for the use of Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs), a principled extension of the familiar Gaussian framework, as they are universal approximators of PDFs whose complexity can be tuned to trade off approximation accuracy against memory and computation. From both mathematical and computational perspectives, GMMs enable high performance and, in many cases, closed form solutions of essential operations in control and measurement.
  The paper presents practical applications within manufacturing and measurement contexts especially circular factory, demonstrating how the GMMs framework supports accurate representation and propagation of measurement uncertainty and offers improved accuracy--compared to the traditional Gaussian framework--while keeping the computations tractable.

</details>


### [206] [CogFormer: Learn All Your Models Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20520)
*Jerry M. Huang,Lukas Schumacher,Niek Stevenson,Stefan T. Radev*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CogFormer：基于Transformer的元摊销框架，用于认知建模，支持跨多种模型结构的参数估计而无需重新训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于神经网络的模拟推理(SBI)在认知建模中面临局限性：当改变参数化、生成函数、先验或设计变量时，需要重新训练模型，这削弱了摊销的优势。需要一种能适应多种模型结构变化的框架。

Method: 提出CogFormer框架，使用基于Transformer的架构，训练一个能在结构相似的模型组合中保持有效的模型。该框架支持改变数据类型、参数、设计矩阵和样本大小，实现跨模型族的参数估计。

Result: 在二元选择、多选择和连续响应的决策模型族中取得了有希望的定量结果。CogFormer能够准确估计跨模型族的参数，摊销偏移最小，表明其作为认知建模工作流催化剂的潜力。

Conclusion: CogFormer是一个强大的元摊销框架，能够加速认知建模工作流，支持研究人员在不同建模假设间快速迭代，而无需为每个变体重新训练模型。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) with neural networks has accelerated and transformed cognitive modeling workflows. SBI enables modelers to fit complex models that were previously difficult or impossible to estimate, while also allowing rapid estimation across large numbers of datasets. However, the utility of SBI for iterating over varying modeling assumptions remains limited: changing parameterizations, generative functions, priors, and design variables all necessitate model retraining and hence diminish the benefits of amortization. To address these issues, we pilot a meta-amortized framework for cognitive modeling which we nickname the CogFormer. Our framework trains a transformer-based architecture that remains valid across a combinatorial number of structurally similar models, allowing for changing data types, parameters, design matrices, and sample sizes. We present promising quantitative results across families of decision-making models for binary, multi-alternative, and continuous responses. Our evaluation suggests that CogFormer can accurately estimate parameters across model families with a minimal amortization offset, making it a potentially powerful engine that catalyzes cognitive modeling workflows.

</details>


### [207] [Interpretable Operator Learning for Inverse Problems via Adaptive Spectral Filtering: Convergence and Discretization Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20602)
*Hang-Cheng Dong,Pengcheng Cheng,Shuhuan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SC-Net是一个在谱域操作的算子学习框架，通过学习基于信噪比的自适应滤波器函数来重加权谱系数，解决不适定逆问题，具有离散不变性保证，并在数值实验中达到理论最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决不适定逆问题需要有效的正则化策略来稳定反演过程对抗测量噪声。传统方法如Tikhonov正则化需要启发式参数调优，而标准深度学习方法通常缺乏可解释性和跨分辨率泛化能力。

Method: 提出SC-Net（谱校正网络），在前向算子的谱域中操作，学习一个基于信噪比的逐点自适应滤波器函数来重加权谱系数。该方法具有理论分析，证明其近似连续逆算子并保证离散不变性。

Result: 在1D积分方程上的数值实验表明：1) 达到理论极小极大最优收敛率（O(δ^0.5) for s=p=1.5），匹配理论下界；2) 学习到可解释的锐截止滤波器，优于Oracle Tikhonov正则化；3) 展示零样本超分辨率能力，在粗网格(N=256)训练后能在细网格(达N=2048)上保持稳定重建误差(≈0.23)。

Conclusion: SC-Net在严格的正则化理论和数据驱动的算子学习之间架起了桥梁，提供了一种具有理论保证、可解释且能跨分辨率泛化的逆问题解决方法。

Abstract: Solving ill-posed inverse problems necessitates effective regularization strategies to stabilize the inversion process against measurement noise. While classical methods like Tikhonov regularization require heuristic parameter tuning, and standard deep learning approaches often lack interpretability and generalization across resolutions, we propose SC-Net (Spectral Correction Network), a novel operator learning framework. SC-Net operates in the spectral domain of the forward operator, learning a pointwise adaptive filter function that reweights spectral coefficients based on the signal-to-noise ratio. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that SC-Net approximates the continuous inverse operator, guaranteeing discretization invariance. Numerical experiments on 1D integral equations demonstrate that SC-Net: (1) achieves the theoretical minimax optimal convergence rate ($O(δ^{0.5})$ for $s=p=1.5$), matching theoretical lower bounds; (2) learns interpretable sharp-cutoff filters that outperform Oracle Tikhonov regularization; and (3) exhibits zero-shot super-resolution, maintaining stable reconstruction errors ($\approx 0.23$) when trained on coarse grids ($N=256$) and tested on significantly finer grids (up to $N=2048$). The proposed method bridges the gap between rigorous regularization theory and data-driven operator learning.

</details>


### [208] [LassoFlexNet: Flexible Neural Architecture for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20631)
*Kry Yik Chau Lui,Cheng Chi,Kishore Basu,Yanshuai Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LassoFlexNet：一种结合树模型优势的深度神经网络架构，通过引入五个关键归纳偏置和稀疏特征选择机制，在表格数据上达到或超越树模型性能，同时保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在视觉和语言任务中占主导地位，但在表格数据上通常不如树模型。需要弥合这一差距，将树模型的优势整合到深度学习中。

Method: 提出LassoFlexNet架构，包含五个关键归纳偏置：对无关特征的鲁棒性、轴对齐、局部不规则性、特征异质性和训练稳定性。使用Per-Feature Embeddings评估每个输入的线性和非线性边际贡献，通过Tied Group Lasso机制稀疏选择相关变量。开发Sequential Hierarchical Proximal Adaptive Gradient优化器确保稳定收敛。

Result: 在三个基准测试的52个数据集上，LassoFlexNet匹配或超越了领先的树模型，相对增益高达10%，同时保持了类似Lasso的可解释性。消融研究和理论证明验证了架构的表达能力和对不良旋转不变性的结构破坏。

Conclusion: 通过将树模型的归纳偏置整合到深度学习中，LassoFlexNet成功弥合了表格数据上深度神经网络与树模型之间的性能差距，实现了高性能与可解释性的平衡。

Abstract: Despite their dominance in vision and language, deep neural networks often underperform relative to tree-based models on tabular data. To bridge this gap, we incorporate five key inductive biases into deep learning: robustness to irrelevant features, axis alignment, localized irregularities, feature heterogeneity, and training stability. We propose \emph{LassoFlexNet}, an architecture that evaluates the linear and nonlinear marginal contribution of each input via Per-Feature Embeddings, and sparsely selects relevant variables using a Tied Group Lasso mechanism. Because these components introduce optimization challenges that destabilize standard proximal methods, we develop a \emph{Sequential Hierarchical Proximal Adaptive Gradient optimizer with exponential moving averages (EMA)} to ensure stable convergence. Across $52$ datasets from three benchmarks, LassoFlexNet matches or outperforms leading tree-based models, achieving up to a $10$\% relative gain, while maintaining Lasso-like interpretability. We substantiate these empirical results with ablation studies and theoretical proofs confirming the architecture's enhanced expressivity and structural breaking of undesired rotational invariance.

</details>


### [209] [Sinkhorn Based Associative Memory Retrieval Using Spherical Hellinger Kantorovich Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20656)
*Aratrika Mustafi,Soumya Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种用于经验测度（加权点云）的稠密联想记忆，基于去偏Sinkhorn散度构建Hopfield风格的log-sum-exp能量，通过球形Hellinger Kantorovich梯度流实现检索，具有指数级容量和鲁棒恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联想记忆模型主要处理向量数据，而现实世界数据常以加权点云（经验测度）形式存在。需要开发能够处理概率测度模式的新型联想记忆框架，利用最优传输理论处理测度间的几何关系。

Method: 基于去偏Sinkhorn散度构建Hopfield风格的log-sum-exp能量函数。检索过程定义为球形Hellinger Kantorovich梯度流，同时更新支持点位置和权重。离散化得到确定性算法，使用Sinkhorn势计算重心传输步骤和乘性单纯形重加权。

Result: 在局部分离和PL型条件下证明：盆地不变性、几何收敛到局部极小值、极小值保持接近存储模式。在随机模式模型下，Sinkhorn盆地以高概率互不相交，表明环境维度中具有指数容量。合成高斯点云记忆实验显示相比欧几里得Hopfield基线具有更鲁棒的恢复能力。

Conclusion: 成功构建了处理经验测度的稠密联想记忆框架，将最优传输理论融入联想记忆模型，实现了对加权点云模式的高效检索和指数级存储容量，为处理复杂几何数据提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose a dense associative memory for empirical measures (weighted point clouds). Stored patterns and queries are finitely supported probability measures, and retrieval is defined by minimizing a Hopfield-style log-sum-exp energy built from the debiased Sinkhorn divergence. We derive retrieval dynamics as a spherical Hellinger Kantorovich (SHK) gradient flow, which updates both support locations and weights. Discretizing the flow yields a deterministic algorithm that uses Sinkhorn potentials to compute barycentric transport steps and a multiplicative simplex reweighting. Under local separation and PL-type conditions we prove basin invariance, geometric convergence to a local minimizer, and a bound showing the minimizer remains close to the corresponding stored pattern. Under a random pattern model, we further show that these Sinkhorn basins are disjoint with high probability, implying exponential capacity in the ambient dimension. Experiments on synthetic Gaussian point-cloud memories demonstrate robust recovery from perturbed queries versus a Euclidean Hopfield-type baseline.

</details>


### [210] [High-dimensional online learning via asynchronous decomposition: Non-divergent results, dynamic regularization, and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20696)
*Shixiang Liu,Zhifan Li,Hanming Yang,Jianxin Yin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出异步分解框架，利用摘要统计量构建代理评分函数进行在线学习，解决高维在线学习中误差界随数据批次增加而发散的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维在线学习方法面临误差界或每批样本量随数据批次增加而发散的问题，需要一种能保持稳定误差界的方法。

Method: 提出异步分解框架，利用摘要统计量构建代理评分函数，通过动态正则化迭代硬阈值算法实现稀疏在线优化，计算和内存效率高。

Result: 理论分析表明估计器在所有批次中保持非发散误差界和ℓ₀稀疏性，随着批次积累自适应获得额外增益，达到如同能访问全部历史数据且已知真实支持集的oracle精度。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维在线学习提供了计算和统计效率兼备的解决方案，通过广义线性模型示例进一步验证了理论性质。

Abstract: Existing high-dimensional online learning methods often face the challenge that their error bounds, or per-batch sample sizes, diverge as the number of data batches increases. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous decomposition framework that leverages summary statistics to construct a surrogate score function for current-batch learning. This framework is implemented via a dynamic-regularized iterative hard thresholding algorithm, providing a computationally and memory-efficient solution for sparse online optimization. We provide a unified theoretical analysis that accounts for both the streaming computational error and statistical accuracy, establishing that our estimator maintains non-divergent error bounds and $\ell_0$ sparsity across all batches. Furthermore, the proposed estimator adaptively achieves additional gains as batches accumulate, attaining the oracle accuracy as if the entire historical dataset were accessible and the true support were known. These theoretical properties are further illustrated through an example of the generalized linear model.

</details>


### [211] [Auto-differentiable data assimilation: Co-learning of states, dynamics, and filtering algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20891)
*Melissa Adrian,Daniel Sanz-Alonso,Rebecca Willett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出可自动微分滤波框架，联合学习状态、动力学和滤波算法参数，通过梯度优化从部分噪声观测中学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化算法需要昂贵的参数调优和精确的动力学模型，限制了其实际应用

Method: 提出可自动微分滤波框架，利用理论驱动的损失函数，通过自动微分进行梯度优化，联合学习状态、动力学和滤波参数

Result: 框架在多个科学领域的动力系统中验证有效，包括航空航天工程的Clohessy-Wiltshire方程、大气科学的Lorenz-96系统和系统生物学的广义Lotka-Volterra方程

Conclusion: 该框架为数据同化提供了灵活的学习方法，可根据观测模型、精度要求和计算预算进行定制，具有广泛的应用潜力

Abstract: Data assimilation algorithms estimate the state of a dynamical system from partial observations, where the successful performance of these algorithms hinges on costly parameter tuning and on employing an accurate model for the dynamics. This paper introduces a framework for jointly learning the state, dynamics, and parameters of filtering algorithms in data assimilation through a process we refer to as auto-differentiable filtering. The framework leverages a theoretically motivated loss function that enables learning from partial, noisy observations via gradient-based optimization using auto-differentiation. We further demonstrate how several well-known data assimilation methods can be learned or tuned within this framework. To underscore the versatility of auto-differentiable filtering, we perform experiments on dynamical systems spanning multiple scientific domains, such as the Clohessy-Wiltshire equations from aerospace engineering, the Lorenz-96 system from atmospheric science, and the generalized Lotka-Volterra equations from systems biology. Finally, we provide guidelines for practitioners to customize our framework according to their observation model, accuracy requirements, and computational budget.

</details>


### [212] [Active Inference for Physical AI Agents -- An Engineering Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20927)
*Bert de Vries*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出基于自由能原理的主动推理作为物理AI代理的理论基础，通过反应式消息传递在因子图上实现变分自由能最小化，为资源受限的物理系统提供统一架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI代理（如机器人）在资源受限的真实环境中能力远不如生物代理，需要理论基础来缩小这一差距。

Method: 从概率论出发，通过贝叶斯机器学习、变分推理到主动推理，提出在因子图上通过反应式消息传递实现变分自由能最小化。

Result: 反应式消息传递具有事件驱动、可中断、局部适应等特性，能优雅处理资源波动和异步数据，耦合的主动推理代理可描述为更高层代理，实现跨尺度同构架构。

Conclusion: 主动推理为物理AI代理提供了理论基础和工程架构，通过统一的反应式消息传递机制适应物理操作约束，实现感知、学习、规划和控制的统一。

Abstract: Physical AI agents, such as robots and other embodied systems operating under tight and fluctuating resource constraints, remain far less capable than biological agents in open-ended real-world environments. This paper argues that Active Inference (AIF), grounded in the Free Energy Principle, offers a principled foundation for closing that gap. We develop this argument from first principles, following a chain from probability theory through Bayesian machine learning and variational inference to active inference and reactive message passing. From the FEP perspective, systems that maintain their structural and functional integrity over time can, under suitable assumptions, be described as minimizing variational free energy (VFE), and AIF operationalizes this by unifying perception, learning, planning, and control within a single computational objective. We show that VFE minimization is naturally realized by reactive message passing on factor graphs, where inference emerges from local, parallel computations. This realization is well matched to the constraints of physical operation, including hard deadlines, asynchronous data, fluctuating power budgets, and changing environments. Because reactive message passing is event-driven, interruptible, and locally adaptable, performance degrades gracefully under reduced resources while model structure can adjust online. We further show that, under suitable coupling and coarse-graining conditions, coupled AIF agents can be described as higher-level AIF agents, yielding a homogeneous architecture based on the same message-passing primitive across scales. Our contribution is not empirical benchmarking, but a clear theoretical and architectural case for the engineering community.

</details>


### [213] [Stability of Sequential and Parallel Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20929)
*Debdeep Pati*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 比较坐标上升变分推断的两种算法：顺序算法收敛条件更宽松但较慢，并行算法计算效率高但收敛条件更严格


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示坐标上升变分推断中顺序和并行算法的行为差异，这种差异在数值分析文献中已知但在更复杂模型的变分推断优化文献中尚未充分探索

Method: 聚焦于中等高维线性回归问题，分析顺序算法和并行算法的收敛特性

Result: 顺序算法虽然通常较慢，但在更宽松的条件下具有收敛保证；并行算法常用于促进块更新和提高计算效率

Conclusion: 两种算法在收敛条件上存在显著差异，顺序算法收敛条件更宽松，并行算法计算效率更高但收敛条件更严格

Abstract: We highlight a striking difference in behavior between two widely used variants of coordinate ascent variational inference: the sequential and parallel algorithms. While such differences were known in the numerical analysis literature in simpler settings, they remain largely unexplored in the optimization-focused literature on variational inference in more complex models. Focusing on the moderately high-dimensional linear regression problem, we show that the sequential algorithm, although typically slower, enjoys convergence guarantees under more relaxed conditions than the parallel variant, which is often employed to facilitate block-wise updates and improve computational efficiency.

</details>


### [214] [Hard labels sampled from sparse targets mislead rotation invariant algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20967)
*Avrajit Ghosh,Bin Yu,Manfred Warmuth,Peter Bartlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文指出在逻辑回归中，旋转不变算法在处理硬标签时存在根本性缺陷：即使目标函数是噪声自由的软目标，旋转不变算法（如梯度下降）在稀疏参数情况下会产生Ω((d-1)/n)的额外风险，而非旋转不变算法能达到O(s log d/n)的更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑回归是机器学习中最常见的设置之一，许多分类模型（包括神经网络）都通过逻辑链接函数进行最终预测。在二元逻辑回归中，反馈可以是软标签（对应数据的真实条件概率，如蒸馏中）或采样的硬标签（取值为±1）。作者发现即使在学习噪声自由软目标的理想情况下，旋转不变算法在处理硬标签时也存在根本性问题。

Method: 论文分析了两种标签设置：1）软标签情况：给定样本(x_i, σ(x_i^T w*))，在过约束情况下可以恢复w*；2）硬标签情况：从相同条件分布σ(x_i^T w*)中采样硬标签y_i。证明旋转不变算法（如逻辑损失的梯度下降）在稀疏目标情况下会产生次优性能，而非旋转不变算法（如将w_i重新参数化为u_i v_i的梯度下降）能达到更好的理论界限。

Result: 当目标是s-稀疏时，旋转不变算法（包括梯度下降）的额外风险为Ω((d-1)/n)，而简单的非旋转不变算法可以达到O(s log d/n)的额外风险。这表明在处理硬标签的稀疏逻辑回归问题时，旋转不变性是一个根本性的限制因素。

Conclusion: 旋转不变算法在逻辑回归的硬标签设置中存在根本性缺陷，即使目标函数是噪声自由的。对于稀疏目标，非旋转不变算法在理论上具有更好的性能。这一发现对理解逻辑回归算法的理论性质具有重要意义，特别是在处理硬标签和稀疏参数的情况下。

Abstract: One of the most common machine learning setups is logistic regression. In many classification models, including neural networks, the final prediction is obtained by applying a logistic link function to a linear score. In binary logistic regression, the feedback can be either soft labels, corresponding to the true conditional probability of the data (as in distillation), or sampled hard labels (taking values $\pm 1$). We point out a fundamental problem that arises even in a particularly favorable setting, where the goal is to learn a noise-free soft target of the form $σ(\mathbf{x}^{\top}\mathbf{w}^{\star})$. In the over-constrained case (i.e. the number of samples $n$ exceeds the input dimension $d$) with examples $(\mathbf{x}_i,σ(\mathbf{x}_i^{\top}\mathbf{w}^{\star}))$, it is sufficient to recover $\mathbf{w}^{\star}$ and hence achieve the Bayes risk. However, we prove that when the examples are labeled by hard labels $y_i$ sampled from the same conditional distribution $σ(\mathbf{x}_i^{\top}\mathbf{w}^{\star})$ and $\mathbf{w}^{\star}$ is $s$-sparse, then rotation-invariant algorithms are provably suboptimal: they incur an excess risk $Ω\!\left(\frac{d-1}{n}\right)$, while there are simple non-rotation invariant algorithms with excess risk $O(\frac{s\log d}{n})$. The simplest rotation invariant algorithm is gradient descent on the logistic loss (with early stopping). A simple non-rotation-invariant algorithm for sparse targets that achieves the above upper bounds uses gradient descent on the weights $u_i,v_i$, where now the linear weight $w_i$ is reparameterized as $u_iv_i$.

</details>


### [215] [Gradient Descent with Projection Finds Over-Parameterized Neural Networks for Learning Low-Degree Polynomials with Nearly Minimax Optimal Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21062)
*Yingzhen Yang,Ping Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究通过训练过参数化两层神经网络学习单位球面上低阶球面多项式的问题，提出了GDP算法，显著降低了样本复杂度，达到了接近最优的回归风险率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习低阶球面多项式在机器学习中很重要，但现有方法样本复杂度高。神经网络具有特征学习能力，但需要理论保证来证明其学习低阶多项式的有效性。

Method: 提出梯度下降投影算法（GDP），训练过参数化两层ReLU神经网络。当真实阶数未知时，还提出了可证明的自适应阶数选择算法。

Result: GDP算法样本复杂度为Θ(log(4/δ)·d^{k₀}/ε)，显著优于代表性的Θ(d^{k₀}max{ε^{-2},log d})。网络训练后的非参数回归风险率为log(4/δ)·Θ(d^{k₀}/n)，接近极小极大最优。

Conclusion: 首次通过训练过参数化ReLU神经网络获得了接近最优的风险界，超越了NTK限制，证明了神经网络学习低阶球面多项式的有效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a low-degree spherical polynomial of degree $k_0 = Θ(1) \ge 1$ defined on the unit sphere in $\RR^d$ by training an over-parameterized two-layer neural network with augmented feature in this paper. Our main result is the significantly improved sample complexity for learning such low-degree polynomials. We show that, for any regression risk $\eps \in (0, Θ(d^{-k_0})]$, an over-parameterized two-layer neural network trained by a novel Gradient Descent with Projection (GDP) requires a sample complexity of $n \asymp Θ( \log(4/δ) \cdot d^{k_0}/\eps)$ with probability $1-δ$ for $δ\in (0,1)$, in contrast with the representative sample complexity $Θ(d^{k_0} \max\set{\eps^{-2},\log d})$. Moreover, such sample complexity is nearly unimprovable since the trained network renders a nearly optimal rate of the nonparametric regression risk of the order $\log({4}/δ) \cdot Θ(d^{k_0}/{n})$ with probability at least $1-δ$. On the other hand, the minimax optimal rate for the regression risk with a kernel of rank $Θ(d^{k_0})$ is $Θ(d^{k_0}/{n})$, so that the rate of the nonparametric regression risk of the network trained by GDP is nearly minimax optimal. In the case that the ground truth degree $k_0$ is unknown, we present a novel and provable adaptive degree selection algorithm which identifies the true degree and achieves the same nearly optimal regression rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that a nearly optimal risk bound is obtained by training an over-parameterized neural network with a popular activation function (ReLU) and algorithmic guarantee for learning low-degree spherical polynomials. Due to the feature learning capability of GDP, our results are beyond the regular Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) limit.

</details>


### [216] [Stochastic approximation in non-markovian environments revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21091)
*Vivek Shripad Borkar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文基于作者近期在非马尔可夫环境中的随机逼近研究，探讨了驱动随机过程既非马尔可夫又非遍历的情况，并提出了一个分析框架来理解基于transformer的学习（特别是注意力机制）和持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机逼近理论主要处理马尔可夫或遍历过程，但transformer的注意力机制和持续学习本质上依赖于整个历史，驱动过程既非马尔可夫又非遍历，需要新的理论框架来分析这些现代机器学习方法。

Method: 基于作者在非马尔可夫环境中随机逼近的最新工作，扩展到驱动随机过程同时具有非马尔可夫和非遍历特性的情况，构建了一个统一的分析框架。

Result: 提出了一个能够处理非马尔可夫且非遍历随机过程的分析框架，为理解transformer注意力机制和持续学习提供了理论基础，这些方法都依赖于整个历史信息。

Conclusion: 该分析框架为理解现代机器学习中依赖于整个历史的方法（如transformer注意力机制和持续学习）提供了理论基础，扩展了随机逼近理论到更一般的非马尔可夫且非遍历环境。

Abstract: Based on some recent work of the author on stochastic approximation in non-markovian environments, the situation when the driving random process is non-ergodic in addition to being non-markovian is considered. Using this, we propose an analytic framework for understanding transformer based learning, specifically, the `attention' mechanism, and continual learning, both of which depend on the entire past in principle.

</details>


### [217] [Time-adaptive functional Gaussian Process regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21144)
*MD Ruiz-Medina,AE Madrid,A Torres-Signes,JM Angulo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于经验贝叶斯方法的流形上函数高斯过程回归新框架，利用流形等距群下的协方差核不变性，通过Laplace-Beltrami算子的特征函数实现降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时空随机场背景下，需要发展适用于流形结构的函数高斯过程回归方法，利用流形的几何特性提高回归性能。

Method: 基于经验贝叶斯方法，利用可分希尔伯特空间中紧高斯测度理论，通过流形等距群下协方差核的不变性，借助Laplace-Beltrami算子的特征函数识别测度，使用时变角谱进行降维。

Result: 模拟研究和合成数据应用展示了所提函数回归预测器的有限样本和渐近性质，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的流形上函数高斯过程回归框架在理论和应用上都具有重要意义，为流形数据分析提供了有效的回归工具。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new formulation of functional Gaussian Process regression in manifolds, based on an Empirical Bayes approach, in the spatiotemporal random field context. We apply the machinery of tight Gaussian measures in separable Hilbert spaces, exploiting the invariance property of covariance kernels under the group of isometries of the manifold. The identification of these measures with infinite-product Gaussian measures is then obtained via the eigenfunctions of the Laplace-Beltrami operator on the manifold. The involved time-varying angular spectra constitute the key tool for dimension reduction in the implementation of this regression approach, adopting a suitable truncation scheme depending on the functional sample size. The simulation study and synthetic data application undertaken illustrate the finite sample and asymptotic properties of the proposed functional regression predictor.

</details>


### [218] [Domain Elastic Transform: Bayesian Function Registration for High-Dimensional Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21235)
*Osamu Hirose,Emanuele Rodola*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Domain Elastic Transform (DET)框架，统一几何与功能对齐，解决空间转录组学等新兴科学数据中高维向量值函数在稀疏流形上的配准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非刚性配准分为点集配准（对齐稀疏几何）和图像配准（对齐规则网格上的连续强度场），但新兴科学数据（如空间转录组学）中高维向量值函数（如基因表达）定义在不规则稀疏流形上，现有方法迫使研究者要么牺牲单细胞分辨率进行体素化，要么忽略关键功能信号。

Method: 提出Domain Elastic Transform (DET)，一种无网格概率框架，将数据视为不规则域上的函数，直接配准高维信号而无需分箱。在严格贝叶斯框架下建模，将域变形建模为由联合空间-功能似然引导的弹性运动。方法完全无监督且可扩展，使用特征敏感下采样处理大规模图谱。

Result: 在MERFISH数据上实现92%的拓扑保持，而最先进的最优传输方法表现不佳（<5%）；成功配准跨发育阶段的全胚胎Stereo-seq图谱，涉及大规模和复杂非刚性生长。

Conclusion: DET解决了新兴科学数据中几何与功能对齐的二分法困境，为空间转录组学等领域的配准问题提供了统一框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Nonrigid registration is conventionally divided into point set registration, which aligns sparse geometries, and image registration, which aligns continuous intensity fields on regular grids. However, this dichotomy creates a critical bottleneck for emerging scientific data, such as spatial transcriptomics, where high-dimensional vector-valued functions, e.g., gene expression, are defined on irregular, sparse manifolds. Consequently, researchers currently face a forced choice: either sacrifice single-cell resolution via voxelization to utilize image-based tools, or ignore the critical functional signal to utilize geometric tools. To resolve this dilemma, we propose Domain Elastic Transform (DET), a grid-free probabilistic framework that unifies geometric and functional alignment. By treating data as functions on irregular domains, DET registers high-dimensional signals directly without binning. We formulate the problem within a rigorous Bayesian framework, modeling domain deformation as an elastic motion guided by a joint spatial-functional likelihood. The method is fully unsupervised and scalable, utilizing feature-sensitive downsampling to handle massive atlases. We demonstrate that DET achieves 92\% topological preservation on MERFISH data where state-of-the-art optimal transport methods struggle ($<$5\%), and successfully registers whole-embryo Stereo-seq atlases across developmental stages -- a task involving massive scale and complex nonrigid growth. The implementation of DET is available on {https://github.com/ohirose/bcpd} (since Mar, 2025).

</details>


### [219] [Accelerate Vector Diffusion Maps by Landmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21247)
*Sing-Yuan Yeh,Yi-An Wu,Hau-Tieng Wu,Mao-Pei Tsui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出LA-VDM算法，通过地标约束加速向量扩散映射，采用两阶段归一化处理非均匀采样密度，在流形框架下能准确恢复平行传输，收敛于连接拉普拉斯算子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量扩散映射(VDM)框架基于图连接拉普拉斯算子，能捕捉复杂数据集中的成对连接关系，但计算效率有待提升。需要一种加速方法，同时能有效处理数据点和地标点的非均匀采样密度问题

Method: 提出LA-VDM（地标加速向量扩散映射）算法：1）采用地标约束加速VDM框架；2）引入新颖的两阶段归一化方法，分别处理数据和地标集的非均匀采样密度；3）在具有框架丛结构的流形模型下，通过地标约束扩散从点云中准确恢复平行传输

Result: 理论证明：LA-VDM能渐近收敛于连接拉普拉斯算子；实验验证：在模拟数据集上展示了LA-VDM的性能和准确性，并成功应用于非局部图像去噪任务

Conclusion: LA-VDM算法有效加速了向量扩散映射框架，通过两阶段归一化解决了非均匀采样密度问题，在流形框架下能准确恢复几何结构，具有理论保证和实际应用价值

Abstract: We propose a landmark-constrained algorithm, LA-VDM (Landmark Accelerated Vector Diffusion Maps), to accelerate the Vector Diffusion Maps (VDM) framework built upon the Graph Connection Laplacian (GCL), which captures pairwise connection relationships within complex datasets. LA-VDM introduces a novel two-stage normalization that effectively address nonuniform sampling densities in both the data and the landmark sets. Under a manifold model with the frame bundle structure, we show that we can accurately recover the parallel transport with landmark-constrained diffusion from a point cloud, and hence asymptotically LA-VDM converges to the connection Laplacian. The performance and accuracy of LA-VDM are demonstrated through experiments on simulated datasets and an application to nonlocal image denoising.

</details>


### [220] [Closed-form conditional diffusion models for data assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21291)
*Brianna Binder,Assad Oberai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于封闭形式条件扩散模型的数据同化方法，利用核密度估计建模状态与观测的联合分布，在非线性系统中优于传统滤波方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化方法在处理非线性、非高斯分布时存在局限，扩散模型在近似复杂概率分布方面具有优势，但通常需要训练神经网络。本文旨在利用扩散模型的分析可处理性，开发无需系统显式知识的黑箱数据同化方法

Method: 使用核密度估计建模状态变量与观测数据的联合分布，利用扩散模型分数函数的解析可处理性，通过反演噪声注入过程实现数据同化。该方法支持黑箱操作，无需系统模型和观测过程的显式知识

Result: 在Lorenz-63和Lorenz-96等中等维度的非线性数据同化问题上进行测试，当使用中小规模集合时，该方法优于广泛使用的集合卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波

Conclusion: 提出的封闭形式条件扩散模型为数据同化提供了有效框架，结合了扩散模型处理复杂分布的优势和黑箱操作的灵活性，在非线性系统中展现出优于传统滤波方法的性能

Abstract: We propose closed-form conditional diffusion models for data assimilation. Diffusion models use data to learn the score function (defined as the gradient of the log-probability density of a data distribution), allowing them to generate new samples from the data distribution by reversing a noise injection process. While it is common to train neural networks to approximate the score function, we leverage the analytical tractability of the score function to assimilate the states of a system with measurements. To enable the efficient evaluation of the score function, we use kernel density estimation to model the joint distribution of the states and their corresponding measurements. The proposed approach also inherits the capability of conditional diffusion models of operating in black-box settings, i.e., the proposed data assimilation approach can accommodate systems and measurement processes without their explicit knowledge. The ability to accommodate black-box systems combined with the superior capabilities of diffusion models in approximating complex, non-Gaussian probability distributions means that the proposed approach offers advantages over many widely used filtering methods. We evaluate the proposed method on nonlinear data assimilation problems based on the Lorenz-63 and Lorenz-96 systems of moderate dimensionality and nonlinear measurement models. Results show the proposed approach outperforms the widely used ensemble Kalman and particle filters when small to moderate ensemble sizes are used.

</details>


### [221] [Generalized Discrete Diffusion from Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21342)
*Oussama Zekri,Théo Uscidda,Nicolas Boullé,Anna Korba*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: GDDS是一个统一的离散扩散建模框架，支持任意噪声过程，通过快照潜在变量简化训练，在大规模离散生成任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散方法在噪声过程选择上受限，训练效率低，且在大规模离散生成任务中表现不如自回归模型。需要更灵活、高效的统一框架。

Method: 提出基于均匀化的前向噪声过程实现快速任意破坏，通过快照潜在变量推导简化的ELBO，使用标准生成架构进行高效训练。

Result: 在大词汇量离散生成任务中，GDDS在训练效率和生成质量上超越现有离散扩散方法，首次在该规模上击败自回归模型。

Conclusion: GDDS提供了一个统一的离散扩散建模框架，具有更大的噪声过程灵活性、更高的训练效率和更好的生成性能，为大规模离散生成任务提供了新方案。

Abstract: We introduce Generalized Discrete Diffusion from Snapshots (GDDS), a unified framework for discrete diffusion modeling that supports arbitrary noising processes over large discrete state spaces. Our formulation encompasses all existing discrete diffusion approaches, while allowing significantly greater flexibility in the choice of corruption dynamics. The forward noising process relies on uniformization and enables fast arbitrary corruption. For the reverse process, we derive a simple evidence lower bound (ELBO) based on snapshot latents, instead of the entire noising path, that allows efficient training of standard generative modeling architectures with clear probabilistic interpretation. Our experiments on large-vocabulary discrete generation tasks suggest that the proposed framework outperforms existing discrete diffusion methods in terms of training efficiency and generation quality, and beats autoregressive models for the first time at this scale. We provide the code along with a blog post on the project page : \href{https://oussamazekri.fr/gdds}{https://oussamazekri.fr/gdds}.

</details>


### [222] [CoNBONet: Conformalized Neuroscience-inspired Bayesian Operator Network for Reliability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21678)
*Shailesh Garg,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CoNBONet：一种受神经科学启发的代理模型，用于非线性动力系统在随机激励下的时变可靠性分析，提供快速、节能且具有理论保证的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法（如蒙特卡洛模拟）需要重复调用计算昂贵的数值求解器，导致计算瓶颈。现有代理模型（如高斯过程、多项式混沌展开等）在处理高维时变可靠性问题时面临可扩展性挑战。

Method: CoNBONet结合了深度算子网络的表达能力与受神经科学启发的神经元模型，实现快速低功耗推理。通过分割保形预测提供具有理论保证的校准不确定性量化，并采用算子学习范式将输入函数映射到系统响应轨迹。

Result: 在各种非线性动力系统上的验证表明，CoNBONet保持了预测保真度，实现了失效概率的可靠覆盖，使其成为工程设计中稳健且可扩展的可靠性分析工具。

Conclusion: CoNBONet为非线性动力系统的时变可靠性分析提供了一种快速、节能且具有理论保证的替代方案，解决了传统方法的计算瓶颈和现有代理模型的可扩展性限制。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis of nonlinear dynamical systems under stochastic excitations is a critical yet computationally demanding task. Conventional approaches, such as Monte Carlo simulation, necessitate repeated evaluations of computationally expensive numerical solvers, leading to significant computational bottlenecks. To address this challenge, we propose \textit{CoNBONet}, a neuroscience-inspired surrogate model that enables fast, energy-efficient, and uncertainty-aware reliability analysis, providing a scalable alternative to techniques such as Monte Carlo simulations. CoNBONet, short for \textbf{Co}nformalized \textbf{N}euroscience-inspired \textbf{B}ayesian \textbf{O}perator \textbf{Net}work, leverages the expressive power of deep operator networks while integrating neuroscience-inspired neuron models to achieve fast, low-power inference. Unlike traditional surrogates such as Gaussian processes, polynomial chaos expansions, or support vector regression, that may face scalability challenges for high-dimensional, time-dependent reliability problems, CoNBONet offers \textit{fast and energy-efficient inference} enabled by a neuroscience-inspired network architecture, \textit{calibrated uncertainty quantification with theoretical guarantees} via split conformal prediction, and \textit{strong generalization capability} through an operator-learning paradigm that maps input functions to system response trajectories. Validation of the proposed CoNBONet for various nonlinear dynamical systems demonstrates that CoNBONet preserves predictive fidelity, and achieves reliable coverage of failure probabilities, making it a powerful tool for robust and scalable reliability analysis in engineering design.

</details>


### [223] [Structural Concentration in Weighted Networks: A Class of Topology-Aware Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21918)
*L. Riso,M. G. Zoia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个统一框架，用于测量嵌入交互网络中的加权系统的集中度，该框架同时考虑权重分布和网络拓扑结构，超越了传统仅关注权重分散度的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中度指标（如赫芬达尔-赫希曼指数）只关注权重分布，忽略了元素间交互关系的网络拓扑结构。在现实系统中，权重相同的元素可能因网络连接方式不同而表现出不同的集中程度，需要同时考虑权重和网络结构。

Method: 提出网络集中度指数（NCI）作为核心基准，定义为归一化的二次型，衡量沿观测网络链接实现的潜在加权互连比例。在此基础上构建了灵活的扩展类别，包括加权、密度调整、零模型、度约束、数据变换和多层变体等。

Result: 理论分析表明该指数族具有归一化、不变性和可解释性等关键性质，能够评估不同维度的依赖性（强度、高阶交互、极端事件）。实证和模拟证据显示，具有相同权重分布的系统可能因网络拓扑不同而表现出显著不同的结构集中度。

Conclusion: 该框架为经济、金融和复杂系统中加权元素的网络交互提供了统一的集中度测量方法，能够捕捉传统指标忽略的网络拓扑信息，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified framework for measuring concentration in weighted systems embedded in networks of interactions. While traditional indices such as the Herfindahl-Hirschman Index capture dispersion in weights, they neglect the topology of relationships among the elements receiving those weights. To address this limitation, we introduce a family of topology-aware concentration indices that jointly account for weight distributions and network structure. At the core of the framework lies a baseline Network Concentration Index (NCI), defined as a normalized quadratic form that measures the fraction of potential weighted interconnection realized along observed network links. Building on this foundation, we construct a flexible class of extensions that modify either the interaction structure or the normalization benchmark, including weighted, density-adjusted, null-model, degree-constrained, transformed-data, and multi-layer variants. This family of indices preserves key properties such as normalization, invariance, and interpretability, while allowing concentration to be evaluated across different dimensions of dependence, including intensity, higher-order interactions, and extreme events. Theoretical results characterize the indices and establish their relationship with classical concentration and network measures. Empirical and simulation evidence demonstrate that systems with identical weight distributions may exhibit markedly different levels of structural concentration depending on network topology, highlighting the additional information captured by the proposed framework. The approach is broadly applicable to economic, financial, and complex systems in which weighted elements interact through networks.

</details>


### [224] [MAGPI: Multifidelity-Augmented Gaussian Process Inputs for Surrogate Modeling from Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22050)
*Atticus Rex,Elizabeth Qian,David Peterson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种新的多保真度高斯过程回归方法，通过低保真度数据定义额外特征来增强输入空间，结合了协同克里金和自回归估计器的优点，在多个测试问题上实现了更高的预测精度和更低的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程和科学应用中，获取高保真度训练数据通常成本高昂且数量有限，导致学习的代理模型不可靠。虽然低保真度模型（如简化物理模型或粗网格）成本较低，但精度不足。多保真度机器学习旨在同时利用高、低保真度数据，学习比低保真度模型更准确、比高保真度模型更便宜的代理模型。

Method: 提出一种新的多保真度高斯过程回归训练方法，使用低保真度数据定义额外的特征来增强学习模型的输入空间。该方法结合了现有多保真度GPR方法中协同克里金和自回归估计器的理想特性。

Result: 在多个测试问题上的数值实验表明，相对于现有技术，该方法实现了更高的预测精度和更低的计算成本。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将协同克里金和自回归估计器的优点结合起来，为多保真度高斯过程回归提供了一种有效的新方法，能够在有限高保真度数据的情况下提高代理模型的准确性和计算效率。

Abstract: Supervised machine learning describes the practice of fitting a parameterized model to labeled input-output data. Supervised machine learning methods have demonstrated promise in learning efficient surrogate models that can (partially) replace expensive high-fidelity models, making many-query analyses, such as optimization, uncertainty quantification, and inference, tractable. However, when training data must be obtained through the evaluation of an expensive model or experiment, the amount of training data that can be obtained is often limited, which can make learned surrogate models unreliable. However, in many engineering and scientific settings, cheaper \emph{low-fidelity} models may be available, for example arising from simplified physics modeling or coarse grids. These models may be used to generate additional low-fidelity training data. The goal of \emph{multifidelity} machine learning is to use both high- and low-fidelity training data to learn a surrogate model which is cheaper to evaluate than the high-fidelity model, but more accurate than any available low-fidelity model. This work proposes a new multifidelity training approach for Gaussian process regression which uses low-fidelity data to define additional features that augment the input space of the learned model. The approach unites desirable properties from two separate classes of existing multifidelity GPR approaches, cokriging and autoregressive estimators. Numerical experiments on several test problems demonstrate both increased predictive accuracy and reduced computational cost relative to the state of the art.

</details>
